# Native authorization findings and source comparisons

- **Status:** active
- **Owning queue item:** [ADOPT-002](active-work.md#adopt-002)
- **Completion / archival evidence:** source navigation prepared for B01 through B17, B19 and B28; B18 and B22 remain pending

The normal reviewer ledger has **41 open findings: ten HIGH, twenty-nine MEDIUM and two LOW**. Their history is preserved while the operator-requested [remediation](native-findings-remediation.md) proceeds. This file records locations and reading commands; it does not accept or dismiss a finding or record independent review or operator security acceptance. Earlier counts of seven omitted older ledger entries.

Within the earlier 26 findings, 23 have technical correction evidence in the
[current remediation record](../../generated/native-checkpoints/finding-remediation.json).
The other three of that earlier group are indexed below. Their instruction correction and contained evaluator controls are local changes. The earlier shared-adapter checkpoint passed 1,259 batches across 68 libraries and preserved 270 runtime package files. Those are historical counts. The current private candidate has 71 libraries and 276 readable package files. Its per-child receipts retain their recorded scope. Current combined acceptance, normal publication and operator review remain required.

## Read original and generated source side by side

From the repository root, verify every readable source copy against its accepted cache entry and byte digest:

```sh
node tools/native-rewrite/review-native-sources.mjs
```

CI runs the same check. To refresh the review copies after normal source admission, run the command with `--write`. Review copies supply no implementation authority and must never be patched by hand.

Then define this shell helper. Every `pair` command below supplies exact original and generated ranges. A `diff` status of 1 means the text differs and is expected. Read both branches and their callers; textual comparison is not an authorization verdict.

```sh
MAIN=ba43c5b28cc0620472b4a09ab699fbc529c1afcb
ORIGINAL_PREFIX=components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation
GENERATED_ROOT=generated/native-review-source
pair() {
  diff -y -W 240 \
    <(git show "$MAIN:$ORIGINAL_PREFIX/$1" | nl -ba | sed -n "$2,${3}p") \
    <(nl -ba "$GENERATED_ROOT/$4" | sed -n "$5,${6}p")
}
```

For B19 and the nine refreshed workspace-admission rows, use the current-v2 main
revision explicitly. This helper reads the same materialized source directory.

```sh
pair19() {
  diff -y -W 240 \
    <(git show "c725c966d1ebe94a912454ded2593a254b8faab4:$ORIGINAL_PREFIX/$1" | nl -ba | sed -n "$2,${3}p") \
    <(nl -ba "$GENERATED_ROOT/$4" | sed -n "$5,${6}p")
}
```

### Read the exact review-time files

Source commands below use reachable rebased ancestors where an earlier review commit was rewritten, or exact review-only historical files under `generated/native-review-history`. Every substituted file has the identical Git blob; `manifest.json` records each historical revision, path and SHA-256. Historical review identifiers and finding dispositions remain unchanged. A fresh checkout of this PR can read these commands without a local backup branch. Historical instructions are evidence for reading, with no implementation or workflow authority.

For each undecided finding the command uses the commit reviewed when that ID was recorded. This also works for a file later removed or revised.

### Constant validation patterns

The returned path-ID and session-key patterns are defined at original `lib/v2/validate.ts:137` through 160 and materialized `field_validation/create_validators.js:8` through 11. Read both definitions with `pair lib/v2/validate.ts 137 160 field_validation/create_validators.js 8 11`. The b64url and list operations are shown separately above.

## Complete operation and guard-site catalog

This catalog includes the remaining admission, authorization, validation and limit operations, plus adjacent conditional behavior needed to inspect them. Within each original operation, every indexed conditional, throw, policy/validation call and security option is listed by its exact line. The generated column lists all guard sites in the selected comparison operation. Split or inlined operations use the full target module. Reading scopes are deliberately broader than a single guard so factored checks and their call sites remain visible. The grouped sites are not a claim of one-to-one syntax or semantic equivalence.

Use `pair` with each row's original and generated range. Main and generated location prefixes are defined above. A group with multiple target operations has a separate command for each. Original helper modules introduced on this branch are not invented as main paths: their original operations are indexed in `lib/config.ts`, `lib/v2/config.ts`, `lib/v2/core.mts`, `lib/v2/db.ts`, `lib/secret.ts`, and the original identity modules. Main's original spend parsers live in its `spend/` directory, not in the new public binding files.

## Schema and input-data constraints

Read the original SQL constraints as well as executable guards. `pair lib/v2/db.ts 120 1105 storage_schema/change_programs.js 1 34` starts the comparison; read the complete materialized `change_programs.js` for every migration string. It contains the declared schema program data, including constraints, foreign keys, indexes and migration statements. The runtime migration and transaction pairs above show where those programs are applied. The original source function and generated constant may have different physical wrapping, so a single source line can contain several SQL statements.

The B13 disposable-domain table is an immutable input, not a generated security primitive. Compare the B23 asset digest and the `isDisposable` / `refuseDisposable` operations. Cryptographic and provider libraries remain caller-supplied resources; the generated signature, token and policy call sites are the locations for reviewing their use.

## Security-relevant retained adapters

The approved supporting classification retains these checks in their original target adapters. They have no relocated generated counterpart. Main means the pinned revision above; current means source checkpoint `c4b853866ff5e130fa294f8e70fe5c0530e87aa4`, whose runtime bytes are unchanged. Both sets are readable with `git show REV:components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/PATH | nl -ba`. The exact 280-path membership and hashes are recorded in the approved retention list.

## Review state

The authoritative ledger remains the source of finding status. All twenty IDs above are open, with zero overrides and no accept/dismiss action. Passing functional tests and providing readable generated source do not decide those findings. Independent review and actual operator security acceptance belong to the operator.

The operation catalog includes 522 original operation groups and 2381 guard, call and option sites. This count describes navigation coverage, not a count of security findings or independently accepted checks.

## B17 metrics and retention source comparisons

These additional locations refer to the separately admitted B17 packages in the
58-library candidate. They were prepared before full application and image
acceptance, which now pass. Normal publication remains pending. No finding is accepted or dismissed by this index.
Original main remains `ba43c5b28cc0620472b4a09ab699fbc529c1afcb`. Use the `pair` helper above after materializing
the current complete manifest into `_build/native-review-source`.

The unchanged original caller admission remains in B12 and B18/B19. Metrics
checks include explicit letter verification, bounded references, duplicates,
date selection and content-free report populations. Retention enforces its
existing workspace/person SQL scope, membership and deadline branches, optional
tables, dry-run rollback and scheduling/error behavior. Inspect the complete
SQL-bearing operation ranges, including queries whose scope is inside SQL text.
The supplied test-identity gate and purge implementation retain their B12
source locations. These operations do not create an additional authenticated
principal admission or rate limiter.

## Latest three findings: read the original and correction together

These findings concern workflow instructions and evaluators introduced by the migration. There is no earlier main application authorization check to invent for them. Their application route checks are already indexed in B19 above. The historical evaluator is authored verification source, not a materialized generated product implementation.

```sh
P=.literate/native-projects/local-server-sessions-directives-routes/skills/agent/develop-in-production-workflow/staging/SKILL.md
diff -y -W 240 \
  <(git show "06b3ac8bb3c1f70a529338db056a89362461d27b:$P" | nl -ba | sed -n '13,25p') \
  <(nl -ba "$P" | sed -n '13,27p')
nl -ba AGENTS.md | sed -n '6,12p'

P=.literate/native-projects/local-server-goals-deliberation-routes/verification/acceptance
# Exact historical host-function crossing, preserved verbatim in the contained verifier.
git show "06b3ac8bb3c1f70a529338db056a89362461d27b:$P/server-goals-deliberation-routes-harness-v4.cjs" | nl -ba | sed -n '68,80p'
rg -n 'historicalVerifier|function runSelected|spawnSync|network=none' "$P/server-goals-deliberation-routes-contained-v1.cjs"

P=.literate/native-projects/local-server-workspace-admin-routes/verification/acceptance
git show "06b3ac8bb3c1f70a529338db056a89362461d27b:$P/server-workspace-admin-routes-harness-v3.cjs" | nl -ba | sed -n '68,80p;107,118p'
rg -n 'historicalVerifier|function runSelected|spawnSync|network=none' "$P/server-workspace-admin-routes-contained-v1.cjs"

# Shared readable adapter and end-to-end controls. The controls use only newly owned public canaries.
nl -ba tools/native-rewrite/contained-oracle-template.cjs
node tools/native-rewrite/test-contained-oracle.mjs
```

All 26 findings remain undecided. Technical correction evidence does not constitute operator acceptance or dismissal. Historical qualification receipts are preserved. Current acceptance through the new evaluator is required before the next integrated candidate can be published.

## Structural assembly acceptance correction

The preserved 70-library run fixed the trust punctuation check but stopped at
1,908 of 1,909 passing checks. A contained evaluator hit its 50-second deadline
inside the output-recovery test before the expected output-path rejection.
The current two structural tests use the existing self-contained provider pair.
They preserve output restoration and the unrelated conflicting file, and require
the actual incorrect-result mismatch rather than accepting any thrown error.
The focused controls pass. All verifier timeouts, sandbox bounds, generated
library bytes and 26 undecided findings are unchanged. Current full acceptance
and normal publication remain required. This changes no relocated application
permission, validation or rate-limit check.

## Container acceptance transport

The main Docker build has no nested Docker runtime. The native migration's
contained independent acceptance therefore runs on the owning host. The build
receives the accepted bundle digest outside its context and checks exact file
membership, modes, bytes, native authority and admitted runtime members before
installing web or operator output. These are new assembly input checks in
`scripts/consume-container-inputs.mjs`; they do not relocate or replace a main
application authorization check. The runtime image excludes the prepared bundle,
source caches, verifiers and assembly helpers. Ten actual-bundle controls cover
normal consumption, missing and changed seals, changed files or authority,
extra files, symlinks, recomputed metadata with unadmitted source, output
conflicts and linked output parents. All 26 findings remain undecided.

Read both the original preparation checks and the new transport guard:

```sh
nl -ba components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/scripts/native-libraries.mjs | sed -n '45,195p'
nl -ba components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/scripts/consume-container-inputs.mjs
nl -ba components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/scripts/prepare-container.mjs
nl -ba components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/scripts/test-container-inputs.mjs
```
